kept = resolveOptions(base, {});\\n  assert.deepEqual(kept, {a: null, b: false, c: 0, d: ''});\\n});\\n\\ntest('resolveOptions drops undefined override values not present in base', () =\\u003e {\\n  const base = {a: 1};\\n  const override = {a: undefined, b: undefined};\\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1});\\n});\\n\\ntest('resolveOptions keeps base values for undefined override keys', () =\\u003e {\\n  const base = {a: 1, b: 2};\\n  const override = {a: undefined, c: 3};\\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2, c: 3});\\n});\\n\\ntest('resolveOptions ignores inherited keys', () =\\u003e {\\n  const base = {a: 1};\\n  const inheritedBase = Object.create({inheritedBase: 'x'});\\n  Object.assign(inheritedBase, {a: 1});\\n  const override = Object.create({inheritedOverride: 'y'});\\n  Object.assign(override, {b: 2});\\n\\n  const result = resolveOptions(inheritedBase, override);\\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2});\\n  assert.equal('inheritedBase' in result, false);\\n  assert.equal('inheritedOverride' in result, false);\\n});\\n\\ntest('resolveOptions returns a new object and never mutates inputs', () =\\u003e {\\n  const base = {a: 1};\\n  const override = {b: 2};\\n  const before = {a: base.a, b: override.b};\\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\\n  assert.notEqual(result, base);\\n  assert.notEqual(result, override);\\n  assert.deepEqual(base, before);\\n  assert.deepEqual(override, before);\\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2});\\n});\\n\"}"

override', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1, b: 2};\n  const override = {b: 3, c: 4};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 3, c: 4});\n 4});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions preserves null, false, zero and empty string', () => {\n  const base = {a: null, b: false, c: 0, d: ''};\n  const override = {a: 1, b: 2, c: 3, d: 4};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2, c: 3, d: 4});\n\n  const kept = resolveOptions(base, {});\n  assert.deepEqual(kept, {a: null, b: false, c: 0, d: ''});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions drops undefined override values not present in base', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1};\n  const override = {a: undefined, b: undefined};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions keeps base values for undefined override keys', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1, b: 2};\n  const override = {a: undefined, c: 3};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2, c: 3});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions ignores inherited keys', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1};\n  const inheritedBase = Object.create({inheritedBase: 'x'});\n  Object.assign(inheritedBase, {a: 1});\n  const override = Object.create({inheritedOverride: 'y'});\n override', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1, b: 2};\n  const override = {b: 3, c: 4};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 3, c: 4});\n  assert.notEqual(result, base);\n  assert.notEqual(result, override);\n  // inputs are not mutated\n  assert.deepEqual(base, {a: 1, b: 2});\n  assert.deepEqual(override, {b: 3, c: 4});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions preserves null, false, zero and empty string', () => {\n  const base = {a: null, b: false, c: 0, d: ''};\n  const override = {a: 1, b: 2, c: 3, d: 4};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2, c: 3, d: 4});\n\n  const kept = resolveOptions(base, {});\n  assert.deepEqual(kept, {a: null, b: false, c: 0, d: ''});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions drops undefined override values not present in base', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1};\n  const override = {a: undefined, b: undefined};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions keeps base values for undefined override keys', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1, b: 2};\n  const override = {a: undefined, c: 3};\n  const result = resolveOptions(base, override);\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2, c: 3});\n});\n\ntest('resolveOptions ignores inherited keys', () => {\n  const base = {a: 1};\n  const inheritedBase = Object.create({inheritedBase: 'x'});\n  Object.assign(inheritedBase, {a: 1});\n  const override = Object.create({inheritedOverride: 'y'});\n  Object.assign(override, {b: 2});\n\n  const result = resolveOptions(inheritedBase, override);\n  assert.deepEqual(result, {a: 1, b: 2});\n  assert.equal('inheritedBase' in result, false);\n
